```
    fn fit_surface(
        mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
        face_list: &[usize],
        vertex_list: &[usize],
        verbosity: u8,
    ) -> Option<Self::Fit> {
        // Sorted, deduplicated vertex list: fitting sums must iterate in a
        // deterministic order (float addition is non-associative; HashSet
        // order varies per process and produced run-to-run fit differences).
        let mut vertex_set: Vec<usize> = vertex_list.to_vec();
        vertex_set.sort_unstable();
        vertex_set.dedup();

        // Try the cheap 4-DOF cylinder fit first. If it is essentially exact
        // (max vertex error at CAD-mesh precision), skip the much more
        // expensive cone LM entirely: a cone could at best tie, and ties
        // select the cylinder anyway. This roughly halves fit cost on
        // cylinder-dominated meshes.
        let cyl_result = fit_cylinder(face_list, &vertex_set, &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices);
        let cyl_fit = cyl_result.map(|(origin, dir, radius)| {
            ConeFit { apex: origin, axis_direction: dir, half_angle: 0.0, radius, convex: None }
        });

        // Compute max vertex error and max surface (centroid) error for cylinder fit
        let (cyl_vtx_max, cyl_sfc_max) = if let Some(ref fit) = cyl_fit {
            let vtx = vertex_list.iter().map(|&vi| {
                let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
                vertex_to_cylinder_distance(v, &fit.apex, &fit.axis_direction, fit.radius).abs()
            }).fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
            let sfc = face_list.iter().map(|&fi| {
                let c = super::face_centroid(&mesh.faces[fi], &mesh.vertices);
                let v = MeshVertex::from_xyz(c[0], c[1], c[2]);
                vertex_to_cylinder_distance(&v, &fit.apex, &fit.axis_direction, fit.radius).abs()
            }).fold(0.0_f64, f64::max);
            (Some(vtx), Some(sfc))
        } else {
            (None, None)
        };
        let _ = cyl_sfc_max; // used only for logging below

        const EXACT_CYLINDER_VTX_ERR: f64 = 1e-6; // mm; CAD-mesh vertex precision
        if let (Some(ref fit), Some(vtx)) = (&cyl_fit, cyl_vtx_max) {
            if vtx <= EXACT_CYLINDER_VTX_ERR {
                if verbosity >= 4 {
                    eprintln!(
                        "      cylinder fit exact (radius={:.6e}, max_vtx_err={:.6e}); skipping cone fit",
                        fit.radius, vtx
                    );
                }
                return cyl_fit;
            }
        }

        // Try cone fit
        let cone_result = fit_cone(face_list, &vertex_set, &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices);
        let cone_fit = cone_result.and_then(|(apex, dir, ha)| {
            let ha_deg = ha.to_degrees();
            if !(MIN_HALF_ANGLE_DEG..=MAX_HALF_ANGLE_DEG).contains(&ha_deg) {
            if verbosity >= 4 {
                    eprintln!(
                        "      cone fit rejected: half_angle={:.2}\u{00b0} outside [{:.0}\u{00b0}, {:.0}\u{00b0}]",
                        ha_deg, MIN_HALF_ANGLE_DEG, MAX_HALF_ANGLE_DEG
                    );
                }
                return None;
            }
            // Reject apex too far from mesh
            let bb_diag = super::bounding_box_diagonal(&mesh.vertices);
            let bb_center = {
                let mut min = [f64::INFINITY; 3];
                let mut max = [f64::NEG_INFINITY; 3];
                for v in &mesh.vertices {
                    min[0] = min[0].min(v.x); max[0] = max[0].max(v.x);
                    min[1] = min[1].min(v.y); max[1] = max[1].max(v.y);
                    min[2] = min[2].min(v.z); max[2] = max[2].max(v.z);
                }
                [(min[0]+max[0])/2.0, (min[1]+max[1])/2.0, (min[2]+max[2])/2.0]
            };
            let apex_dist = ((apex[0] - bb_center[0]).powi(2)
                + (apex[1] - bb_center[1]).powi(2)
```

> 
> So instead of separate rungs for cylinder and cone, I can use `ConicalSurface` as a single rung that prefers cylinder when both validate. This gives me a four-rung ladder: Plane, Sphere, Conical (cylinder-or-cone), Torus. The DOF ordering still works—plane at 3, sphere at 4, conical preferring cylinder at 5 but falling back to cone at 6, and torus at 7—so the ladder respects the complexity hierarchy while keeping the dual-fit logic intact.
> 
> For the jet signature, I'll use it to reorder the rungs: a (κ,κ) region tries sphere first, a (κ,0) region tries conical first, and so on. The default order stays DOF-ascending, but the jet prediction moves the likely candidate to the front of the queue. ying sphere first on a planar region could produce a degenerate fit with a huge radius that still validates, which is the real concern. To stay sound with the "simplest-first" principle, I shouldn't allow the jet to override the DOF ordering—I need to respect the hierarchy regardless of what the curvature estimates suggest.
> 
> The jet should work as a pruning mechanism instead: skip rungs whose signature is incompatible with the region, but don't reorder them ahead of simpler ones. For a (κ,κ) region, plane is already incompatible so sphere tries first anyway, and since we still evaluate all remaining rungs as fallbacks, correctness holds as long as we don't skip a rung prematurely.
> 
> The degeneracy risk—where a higher-DOF surface validates a simpler region—is mitigated because the jet won't predict a complex type for a genuinely simple region (a plane reads (0,0), a cylinder reads (κ,0)). So the safest approach is to always try in DOF order (Plane, Sphere, Conical, Torus), use the jet only to prune incompatible rungs, and take the first one that validates. ond rung fails), try next rung. Jet says (κ,0) [curvature present, torsion absent]: Plane and Sphere are ruled out → try Conical. Jet says (κ,κ) [both present]: all three rungs are possible → try Sphere first (simplest), then Conical, then Torus. This strategy is sound because each shape class has a specific signature it satisfies, and the jet constraints correctly filter incompatible candidates before we waste time on validation.
> 
> But I need to be careful with pruning—it's an optimization that could cause correctness issues if too aggressive. If I prune a shape that could actually fit, the region falls back to per-facet handling, which is a miss. So I should only prune when I'm certain a shape can't work, otherwise keep the fallback as a safety net. For large regions like rounded_cube's 10853 faces, fitting the full ladder would be expensive, so I should subsample vertices for the fit, validate against all vertices, and refit with worst offenders if needed to keep costs bounded. 1003	/// Refine surface assignments at region boundaries (DEVELOPMENT_PLAN §2.6
```
1004	/// "Surface refitting"). BFS greedily absorbs border faces that fit within
1005	/// tolerance but belong more naturally to the adjacent surface — e.g. a face
1006	/// one row past a fillet junction absorbed by the plane. The misassignment
1007	/// shifts the mesh region boundary by a row, which shifts reconstructed edge
1008	/// boundary vertices and corner vertices off the true feature curves.
1009	///
1010	/// For each face on a region boundary, compare the max vertex distance to
1011	/// its own surface against each adjacent selected surface, and move the face
1012	/// when an adjacent surface fits at least 2× better. The margin prevents
1013	/// oscillation for true junction faces (whose vertices lie on both surfaces
1014	/// equally). Iterates to a fixpoint (boundaries only ever move locally).
```

```
//! Stage 2.3(a): the whole-region DOF ladder.
//!
//! Try to explain each smooth region (stage 2.2) with ONE surface, ascending
//! degrees of freedom; the first fit that validates wins. Simplest-first
//! resolves degeneracy (any plane also lies on an infinite-radius cylinder; a
//! single facet is exactly a plane) without coverage heuristics.
//!
//! | Rung | Surface  | DOF |
//! |------|----------|-----|
//! | 1    | Plane    | 3   |
//! | 2    | Sphere   | 4   |
//! | 3    | Conical  | 5/6 | (cylinder-or-cone; the fitter prefers cylinder)
//! | 4    | Torus    | 7   |
//!
//! The conical rung fits a cylinder first and only falls to a cone when the
//! cylinder does not validate (`ConicalSurface::fit_surface`), so it covers
//! both the 5-DOF cylinder and 6-DOF cone cases in DOF order.
//!
//! **Validation decides all geometry** (Design Principle #1): every claimed
//! vertex within `--vertex-tolerance`, every claimed face centroid within
//! `--surface-tolerance`, and orientation coherence (every face normal
//! agrees in sign with the surface normal at its centroid — the per-type
//! `convexity_check`, which also fixes the hypothesis `convex` flag). The
//! fitters apply their own numeric-validity guards (cone half-angle bounds,
//! torus `0 < r ≤ R`, sphere radius cap) and return `None` outside them.
//!
//! A region the ladder cannot explain with one surface is a tangent chain
//! (pill capsule, rounded-cube shell, pipe-elbow tube). Decomposing those is
//! stage 2.3(b) scoped competition (not yet implemented). Until then such a
//! region **terminally degrades to per-facet planes** — total coverage can
//! never fail, and a single triangle is exactly a plane.

use crate::config::Config;
use crate::stage1::ConnectedMesh;

use super::bfs::SurfaceType;
use super::conical::ConicalSurface;
use super::planar::{fit_plane, PlanarSurface};
use super::regions::Region;
use super::spherical::SphericalSurface;
use super::toroidal::ToroidalSurface;
use super::{
    face_centroid, ConicalHypothesis, PlanarHypothesis, SelectedSurface, SphericalHypothesis,
    ToroidalHypothesis,
};

use std::collections::HashSet;

/// Regions larger than this many faces are fitted on a deterministic
/// stride subsample (validated against the whole region, refit-augmented on
/// a near-miss). Bounds fit cost on huge regions — which, lacking scoped
/// competition, are the tangent chains that fail the ladder anyway.
const MAX_FIT_FACES: usize = 3000;

/// Bounded refit rounds: after a subsample fit, the worst validation
/// offenders are added to the fit set and the surface is refit, so a large
/// clean surface still validates end to end.
const MAX_REFIT: usize = 3;

/// How many worst-offender faces to fold into the fit set per refit round.
const REFIT_ADD: usize = 32;

/// Give-up threshold for a refit near-miss: if the worst offender exceeds
/// this multiple of `--surface-tolerance`, the region is decisively not this
/// surface and no amount of augmenting will help.
const REFIT_GIVE_UP: f64 = 8.0;

/// The output of stage 2.3(a): committed hypotheses in the typed vectors plus
/// one `SelectedSurface` per committed surface. Regions partition the mesh,
/// so every mesh face lands in exactly one selected surface.
pub(super) struct Classification {
    pub planar: Vec<PlanarHypothesis>,
    pub conical: Vec<ConicalHypothesis>,
    pub spherical: Vec<SphericalHypothesis>,
    pub toroidal: Vec<ToroidalHypothesis>,
    pub selected: Vec<SelectedSurface>,
}

impl Classification {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Classification {
            planar: Vec::new(),
            conical: Vec::new(),
            spherical: Vec::new(),
            toroidal: Vec::new(),
            selected: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    fn push_planar(&mut self, hyp: PlanarHypothesis) {
        self.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Planar(self.planar.len()));
        self.planar.push(hyp);
    }
    fn push_conical(&mut self, hyp: ConicalHypothesis) {
        self.selected
            .push(SelectedSurface::Conical(self.conical.len()));
        self.conical.push(hyp);
    }
    fn push_spherical(&mut self, hyp: SphericalHypothesis) {
        self.selected
            .push(SelectedSurface::Spherical(self.spherical.len()));
        self.spherical.push(hyp);
    }
    fn push_toroidal(&mut self, hyp: ToroidalHypothesis) {
        self.selected
            .push(SelectedSurface::Toroidal(self.toroidal.len()));
        self.toroidal.push(hyp);
    }
}

/// Run the whole-region ladder over every smooth region.
pub(super) fn classify_regions(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    regions: &[Region],
    config: &Config,
) -> Classification {
    let centroids: Vec<[f64; 3]> = mesh
        .faces
        .iter()
        .map(|f| face_centroid(f, &mesh.vertices))
        .collect();

    let mut cls = Classification::new();
    let mut ladder_wins = [0usize; 4]; // plane, sphere, conical, torus
    let mut fallback_regions = 0usize;
    for region in regions {
        if classify_one_region(mesh, region, &centroids, config, &mut cls, &mut ladder_wins) {
            continue;
        }
        // Terminal fallback: per-facet planes (total coverage can never fail).
        fallback_regions += 1;
        for &fi in &region.faces {
            cls.push_planar(facet_plane(mesh, fi));
        }
    }

    if config.verbosity >= 1 {
        eprintln!(
            "  ladder: {} plane, {} sphere, {} cyl/cone, {} torus; {} region(s) → per-facet fallback",
            ladder_wins[0], ladder_wins[1], ladder_wins[2], ladder_wins[3], fallback_regions,
        );
    }
    cls
}

/// Try the DOF ladder on one region; commit the first surface that validates.
/// Returns true on a committed surface, false if the region fell through
/// (caller applies the per-facet fallback).
fn classify_one_region(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    region: &Region,
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
    cls: &mut Classification,
    wins: &mut [usize; 4],
) -> bool {
    let faces = &region.faces;
    let verts = &region.vertices;

    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
        cls.push_planar(hyp);
        wins[0] += 1;
        return true;
    }
    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
        cls.push_spherical(hyp);
        wins[1] += 1;
        return true;
    }
    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
        cls.push_conical(hyp);
        wins[2] += 1;
        return true;
    }
    if let Some(hyp) = fit_validate::<ToroidalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config) {
        cls.push_toroidal(hyp);
        wins[3] += 1;
        return true;
    }
    false
}

/// Fit surface type `S` to a region and validate it against the whole region.
/// Large regions fit on a stride subsample, refit-augmented with the worst
/// validation offenders on a near-miss. Returns the committed hypothesis, or
/// `None` if `S` cannot explain the region within tolerance.
fn fit_validate<S: SurfaceType>(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    region_faces: &[usize],
    region_verts: &[usize],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
) -> Option<S::Hypothesis> {
    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;

    // Initial fit set: the whole region, or a deterministic stride subsample
    // when the region is large.
    let mut fit_faces: Vec<usize> = if region_faces.len() > MAX_FIT_FACES {
        let stride = region_faces.len().div_ceil(MAX_FIT_FACES);
        region_faces.iter().copied().step_by(stride).collect()
    } else {
        region_faces.to_vec()
    };

    for _round in 0..=MAX_REFIT {
        let fit_verts = vertices_of(mesh, &fit_faces);
        let mut fit = S::fit_surface(mesh, &fit_faces, &fit_verts, config.verbosity)?;
        S::determine_convexity(mesh, region_faces, &mut fit);

        // Validate every region face; collect near-miss offenders for a refit.
        let mut offenders: Vec<(f64, usize)> = Vec::new();
        for &fi in region_faces {
            // Orientation coherence: a face whose normal disagrees with the
            // fitted surface's convexity means the region is not one clean
            // surface of type S — no refit rescues that.
            if !S::convexity_check(&fit, mesh, fi) {
                return None;
            }
            let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
            let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
            let mut excess = 0.0_f64;
            for &vi in &face.vertex_indices[..vc] {
                let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
                let d = S::vertex_distance(&fit, &[v.x, v.y, v.z]);
                if d > vtol {
                    excess = excess.max(d - vtol);
                }
            }
            let cd = S::centroid_distance(&fit, &centroids[fi]);
            if cd > stol {
                excess = excess.max(cd - stol);
            }
            if excess > 0.0 {
                offenders.push((excess, fi));
            }
        }

        if offenders.is_empty() {
            return Some(S::make_hypothesis(
                mesh,
                region_faces.to_vec(),
                region_verts.to_vec(),
                &fit,
            ));
        }

        // Near-miss handling: if the worst offender is far out, the region is
        // decisively not this surface. Otherwise fold the worst offenders
        // into the fit set and refit (bounded rounds).
        offenders.sort_by(|a, b| {
            b.0.partial_cmp(&a.0)
                .unwrap()
                .then_with(|| a.1.cmp(&b.1))
        });
        if offenders[0].0 > REFIT_GIVE_UP * stol {
            return None;
        }
        let before = fit_faces.len();
        let existing: HashSet<usize> = fit_faces.iter().copied().collect();
        for &(_, fi) in offenders.iter().take(REFIT_ADD) {
            if !existing.contains(&fi) {
                fit_faces.push(fi);
            }
        }
        fit_faces.sort_unstable();
        fit_faces.dedup();
        if fit_faces.len() == before {
            // No new faces to learn from — cannot improve.
            return None;
        }
    }
    None
}

/// Sorted, deduplicated vertex indices of a face set.
fn vertices_of(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> Vec<usize> {
    let mut v: Vec<usize> = faces
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|&fi| {
            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
            mesh.faces[fi].vertex_indices[..vc].iter().copied()
        })
        .collect();
    v.sort_unstable();
    v.dedup();
    v
}

/// A single-face planar hypothesis (the terminal per-facet fallback).
fn facet_plane(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, fi: usize) -> PlanarHypothesis {
    let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
    let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
    let vertices: Vec<usize> = face.vertex_indices[..vc].to_vec();
    let vertex_set: HashSet<usize> = vertices.iter().copied().collect();
    let (normal, distance) = fit_plane(&[fi], &vertex_set, &mesh.faces, &mesh.vertices);
    let mut error_max = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
    let mut error_min = f64::INFINITY;
    let mut error_abs_sum = 0.0_f64;
    for &vi in &vertices {
        let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
        let d = normal[0] * v.x + normal[1] * v.y + normal[2] * v.z - distance;
        error_max = error_max.max(d);
        error_min = error_min.min(d);
        error_abs_sum += d.abs();
    }
    PlanarHypothesis {
        normal,
        distance,
        faces: vec![fi],
        vertices,
        error_max,
        error_min,
        error_abs_sum,
    }
}

```

```bash
cd .
run() {
  local stl="$1"; local step="$2"; local name="$3"
  if [ ! -f "$step" ]; then echo "SKIP  $name (no ref)"; return; fi
  out=$(./target/debug/brepper "$stl" -o /tmp/out.step --compare "$step" 2>&1)
  ec=$?
  surf=$(echo "$out" | grep "Stage 2.3" | sed 's/.*Region classification: //')
  vol=$(echo "$out" | grep "Compare 4.1: output volume" | tail -1 | sed 's/.*output //')
  final=$(echo "$out" | grep -E "Stage 4.1|Total CPU" | head -1)
  err=$(echo "$out" | grep -iE "error|panic|failed" | head -1)
  printf "%-28s exit=%s | %s | %s %s\n" "$name" "$ec" "${surf:-NO 2.3}" "${vol:-novol}" "${err:+ERR:$err}"
}
for m in cube block_with_hole simple_cylinder simple_sphere hemisphere ball_on_cylinder pipe two_holes spherical_pocket; do
  run "tests/ccad/generated/$m.stl" "tests/ccad/generated/$m.step" "$m"
done
for m in chamfered_cube_10_c1_medium; do
  run "tests/onshape/$m.stl" "tests/onshape/$m.step" "$m"
done
```

```
//! Stage 2.4: resolution & repair.
//!
//! Turns stage 2.3's per-region hypotheses into a strict, connected,
//! validated partition. All operations are fit-validated and deterministic
//! (fixed iteration order — by surface index, smallest first for merges —
//! bounded rounds).
//!
//! - **Boundary relabel** (±1-row polish): move a region-boundary face to an
//!   adjacent surface that fits its vertices at least 2× better. Region
//!   geometry is not refitted — only the membership map changes. Iterates to
//!   a fixpoint (≤ 10 rounds; boundaries only move locally).
//! - **Merge**: absorb a small region S into an adjacent, larger, real
//!   surface T when every vertex of S lies within `--vertex-tolerance` of T's
//!   fitted surface and every centroid within `--surface-tolerance`; refit
//!   the union, revalidate, keep only if valid. Eats junction-circle
//!   singletons and chord-degenerate slivers with a principled criterion —
//!   vertex tolerance, at which "on the same surface" is decisive.
//! - **Connectivity re-split**: relabel/merge can disconnect a surface;
//!   re-split into mesh-connected components (identical fitted geometry on
//!   two non-adjacent regions is legal).
//! - **Invariants** (hard checks before stage 3): total coverage and
//!   per-surface mesh connectivity.
//!
//! Claim resolution (multi-claimed junction rows) is a no-op here until
//! stage 2.3(b) scoped competition exists — the whole-region ladder produces
//! disjoint regions, so no face is claimed twice.

use crate::config::Config;
use crate::stage1::{ConnectedMesh, MeshVertex};

use super::classify::{fit_validate, Classification};
use super::conical::ConicalSurface;
use super::planar::PlanarSurface;
use super::spherical::SphericalSurface;
use super::toroidal::ToroidalSurface;
use super::{
    face_centroid, vertex_distance_to_selected, ConicalHypothesis, PlanarHypothesis,
    SelectedSurface, SphericalHypothesis, ToroidalHypothesis,
};

/// Largest region (in faces) eligible to be absorbed by a merge. Junction
/// singletons and chord-degenerate slivers are small; a real surface is not
/// dissolved into a neighbor.
const MERGE_MAX_SMALL: usize = 16;

/// Repair-operation counts, for logging.
#[derive(Default)]
pub(super) struct RepairStats {
    pub relabeled: usize,
    pub merged: usize,
    pub split: usize,
}

/// Run the stage-2.4 repair sequence in place, then assert the hard
/// partition invariants.
pub(super) fn repair(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &mut Classification, config: &Config) -> RepairStats {
    let centroids: Vec<[f64; 3]> = mesh
        .faces
        .iter()
        .map(|f| face_centroid(f, &mesh.vertices))
        .collect();

    let mut stats = RepairStats::default();
    stats.relabeled = relabel_boundaries(mesh, cls, config);
    stats.merged = merge_regions(mesh, cls, &centroids, config);
    stats.split = resplit_disconnected(mesh, cls);
    drop_empty_surfaces(cls);
    assert_invariants(mesh, cls);
    stats
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Surface accessors
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

fn surface_faces<'a>(cls: &'a Classification, si: usize) -> &'a [usize] {
    match cls.selected[si] {
        SelectedSurface::Planar(i) => &cls.planar[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Conical(i) => &cls.conical[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Spherical(i) => &cls.spherical[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Toroidal(i) => &cls.toroidal[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::BSpline(_) => &[],
    }
}

fn surface_faces_mut<'a>(cls: &'a mut Classification, si: usize) -> &'a mut Vec<usize> {
    match cls.selected[si] {
        SelectedSurface::Planar(i) => &mut cls.planar[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Conical(i) => &mut cls.conical[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Spherical(i) => &mut cls.spherical[i].faces,
        SelectedSurface::Toroidal(i) => &mut cls.toroidal[i].faces,
        // Step 2/3 produce no B-spline hypotheses; this arm is unreachable.
        SelectedSurface::BSpline(_) => unreachable!("no bspline hypotheses in stage 2.3/2.4 yet"),
    }
}

/// Distance from a point to a selected surface's fitted geometry.
fn point_dist(cls: &Classification, si: usize, p: &[f64; 3]) -> f64 {
    let v = MeshVertex::from_xyz(p[0], p[1], p[2]);
    vertex_distance_to_selected(
        &v,
        &cls.selected[si],
        &cls.planar,
        &cls.conical,
        &cls.spherical,
        &cls.toroidal,
        &[],
    )
}

/// Max distance from a face's vertices to a selected surface.
fn face_vertex_dist(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &Classification, si: usize, fi: usize) -> f64 {
    let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
    let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
    face.vertex_indices[..vc]
        .iter()
        .map(|&vi| {
            let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
            point_dist(cls, si, &[v.x, v.y, v.z])
        })
        .fold(0.0_f64, f64::max)
}

/// Build the face → selected-surface map (usize::MAX where unassigned).
fn face_to_surface(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &Classification) -> Vec<usize> {
    let mut map = vec![usize::MAX; mesh.faces.len()];
    for si in 0..cls.selected.len() {
        for &fi in surface_faces(cls, si) {
            map[fi] = si;
        }
    }
    map
}

/// Sorted, deduplicated vertex indices of a face set.
fn vertices_of(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> Vec<usize> {
    let mut v: Vec<usize> = faces
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|&fi| {
            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
            mesh.faces[fi].vertex_indices[..vc].iter().copied()
        })
        .collect();
    v.sort_unstable();
    v.dedup();
    v
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Boundary relabel (port of the old refine_boundary_assignments)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Move region-boundary faces to an adjacent surface that fits them at least
/// 2× better. Geometry is not refitted; only the membership map changes.
fn relabel_boundaries(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &mut Classification, _config: &Config) -> usize {
    let num_faces = mesh.faces.len();
    let mut total_moved = 0usize;

    for _iter in 0..10 {
        let f2s = face_to_surface(mesh, cls);
        let mut moves: Vec<(usize, usize, usize)> = Vec::new(); // (face, from, to)
        for fi in 0..num_faces {
            let si = f2s[fi];
            if si == usize::MAX {
                continue;
            }
            let face = &mesh.faces[fi];
            let vc = face.vertex_count as usize;
            let mut candidates: Vec<usize> = face.neighbors[..vc]
                .iter()
                .filter(|&&ni| ni >= 0)
                .map(|&ni| f2s[ni as usize])
                .filter(|&sj| sj != usize::MAX && sj != si)
                .collect();
            if candidates.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }
            candidates.sort_unstable();
            candidates.dedup();

            let d_own = face_vertex_dist(mesh, cls, si, fi);
            let mut best: Option<(f64, usize)> = None;
            for &sj in &candidates {
                let d = face_vertex_dist(mesh, cls, sj, fi);
                if best.as_ref().is_none_or(|(bd, _)| d < *bd) {
                    best = Some((d, sj));
                }
            }
            if let Some((d_best, sj)) = best {
                if d_best < d_own * 0.5 {
                    moves.push((fi, si, sj));
                }
            }
        }

        if moves.is_empty() {
            break;
        }
        total_moved += moves.len();
        for &(fi, si, _) in &moves {
            surface_faces_mut(cls, si).retain(|&f| f != fi);
        }
        for &(fi, _, sj) in &moves {
            surface_faces_mut(cls, sj).push(fi);
        }
    }

    if total_moved > 0 {
        rebuild_all(mesh, cls);
    }
    total_moved
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Merge
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// A refit union hypothesis, tagged by type so the caller can store it back
/// into the matching typed vector.
enum Refit {
    Planar(PlanarHypothesis),
    Conical(ConicalHypothesis),
    Spherical(SphericalHypothesis),
    Toroidal(ToroidalHypothesis),
}

/// Refit a union of faces with the type of `sel` and validate it end to end.
fn refit_union(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    sel: SelectedSurface,
    faces: &[usize],
    verts: &[usize],
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
) -> Option<Refit> {
    match sel {
        SelectedSurface::Planar(_) => {
            fit_validate::<PlanarSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config).map(Refit::Planar)
        }
        SelectedSurface::Conical(_) => {
            fit_validate::<ConicalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config).map(Refit::Conical)
        }
        SelectedSurface::Spherical(_) => {
            fit_validate::<SphericalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config)
                .map(Refit::Spherical)
        }
        SelectedSurface::Toroidal(_) => {
            fit_validate::<ToroidalSurface>(mesh, faces, verts, centroids, config)
                .map(Refit::Toroidal)
        }
        SelectedSurface::BSpline(_) => None,
    }
}

/// Absorb small regions into adjacent larger real surfaces (fit-validated).
fn merge_regions(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    cls: &mut Classification,
    centroids: &[[f64; 3]],
    config: &Config,
) -> usize {
    let vtol = config.vertex_tolerance_mm;
    let stol = config.surface_tolerance_mm;
    let mut merged = 0usize;

    loop {
        let f2s = face_to_surface(mesh, cls);
        // Smallest surfaces first (then index) — deterministic.
        let mut order: Vec<usize> = (0..cls.selected.len())
            .filter(|&si| !surface_faces(cls, si).is_empty())
            .collect();
        order.sort_by_key(|&si| (surface_faces(cls, si).len(), si));

        let mut did = false;
        for &si in &order {
            let s_faces: Vec<usize> = surface_faces(cls, si).to_vec();
            if s_faces.is_empty() || s_faces.len() > MERGE_MAX_SMALL {
                continue;
            }
            // Adjacent surfaces (real, strictly larger than S).
            let mut neighbors: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
            for &fi in &s_faces {
                let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
                for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
                    if ni >= 0 {
                        let sj = f2s[ni as usize];
                        if sj != usize::MAX && sj != si {
                            neighbors.push(sj);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
            neighbors.sort_unstable();
            neighbors.dedup();

            let s_verts = vertices_of(mesh, &s_faces);
            for &ti in &neighbors {
                let t_len = surface_faces(cls, ti).len();
                if t_len <= 2 || t_len <= s_faces.len() {
                    continue; // T must be a larger, real surface
                }
                // Every S vertex on T within vertex tol, every S centroid
                // within surface tol — "on the same surface" is decisive.
                let verts_ok = s_verts.iter().all(|&vi| {
                    let v = &mesh.vertices[vi];
                    point_dist(cls, ti, &[v.x, v.y, v.z]) <= vtol
                });
                if !verts_ok {
                    continue;
                }
                let cents_ok = s_faces.iter().all(|&fi| point_dist(cls, ti, &centroids[fi]) <= stol);
                if !cents_ok {
                    continue;
                }
                // Refit the union and revalidate; keep only if valid.
                let mut union_faces = surface_faces(cls, ti).to_vec();
                union_faces.extend_from_slice(&s_faces);
                union_faces.sort_unstable();
                union_faces.dedup();
                let union_verts = vertices_of(mesh, &union_faces);
                let sel_t = cls.selected[ti];
                if let Some(refit) =
                    refit_union(mesh, sel_t, &union_faces, &union_verts, centroids, config)
                {
                    store_refit(cls, ti, refit);
                    surface_faces_mut(cls, si).clear();
                    merged += 1;
                    did = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
        if !did {
            break;
        }
    }
    merged
}

/// Store a refit union hypothesis back into the vector for surface `ti`.
fn store_refit(cls: &mut Classification, ti: usize, refit: Refit) {
    match (cls.selected[ti], refit) {
        (SelectedSurface::Planar(i), Refit::Planar(h)) => cls.planar[i] = h,
        (SelectedSurface::Conical(i), Refit::Conical(h)) => cls.conical[i] = h,
        (SelectedSurface::Spherical(i), Refit::Spherical(h)) => cls.spherical[i] = h,
        (SelectedSurface::Toroidal(i), Refit::Toroidal(h)) => cls.toroidal[i] = h,
        _ => unreachable!("refit type must match surface type"),
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Connectivity re-split
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Re-split any surface whose faces are not mesh-connected into one surface
/// per connected component (each an independent copy of the fitted geometry).
fn resplit_disconnected(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &mut Classification) -> usize {
    let mut extra = 0usize;
    let original = cls.selected.len();
    for si in 0..original {
        let faces = surface_faces(cls, si).to_vec();
        if faces.len() < 2 {
            continue;
        }
        let components = connected_components(mesh, &faces);
        if components.len() < 2 {
            continue;
        }
        // Keep the largest component on the original surface; clone the rest.
        let mut comps = components;
        comps.sort_by_key(|c| std::cmp::Reverse(c.len()));
        for comp in comps.iter().skip(1) {
            clone_surface_with_faces(mesh, cls, si, comp);
            extra += 1;
        }
        set_surface_faces(mesh, cls, si, comps[0].clone());
    }
    extra
}

/// Connected components of a face set over shared mesh edges.
fn connected_components(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, faces: &[usize]) -> Vec<Vec<usize>> {
    let member: std::collections::HashSet<usize> = faces.iter().copied().collect();
    let mut seen: std::collections::HashSet<usize> = std::collections::HashSet::new();
    let mut components: Vec<Vec<usize>> = Vec::new();
    for &start in faces {
        if seen.contains(&start) {
            continue;
        }
        let mut comp = Vec::new();
        let mut stack = vec![start];
        seen.insert(start);
        while let Some(fi) = stack.pop() {
            comp.push(fi);
            let vc = mesh.faces[fi].vertex_count as usize;
            for &ni in &mesh.faces[fi].neighbors[..vc] {
                if ni >= 0 {
                    let ni = ni as usize;
                    if member.contains(&ni) && seen.insert(ni) {
                        stack.push(ni);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        comp.sort_unstable();
        components.push(comp);
    }
    components
}

/// Append a new selected surface cloning `si`'s geometry but owning `faces`.
fn clone_surface_with_faces(
    mesh: &ConnectedMesh,
    cls: &mut Classification,
    si: usize,
    faces: &[usize],
) {
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, faces);
    match cls.selected[si] {
        SelectedSurface::Planar(i) => {
            let mut h = cls.planar[i].clone();
            h.faces = faces.to_vec();
            h.vertices = verts;
            cls.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Planar(cls.planar.len()));
            cls.planar.push(h);
        }
        SelectedSurface::Conical(i) => {
            let mut h = cls.conical[i].clone();
            h.faces = faces.to_vec();
            h.vertices = verts;
            cls.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Conical(cls.conical.len()));
            cls.conical.push(h);
        }
        SelectedSurface::Spherical(i) => {
            let mut h = cls.spherical[i].clone();
            h.faces = faces.to_vec();
            h.vertices = verts;
            cls.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Spherical(cls.spherical.len()));
            cls.spherical.push(h);
        }
        SelectedSurface::Toroidal(i) => {
            let mut h = cls.toroidal[i].clone();
            h.faces = faces.to_vec();
            h.vertices = verts;
            cls.selected.push(SelectedSurface::Toroidal(cls.toroidal.len()));
            cls.toroidal.push(h);
        }
        SelectedSurface::BSpline(_) => unreachable!("no bspline hypotheses in stage 2.3/2.4 yet"),
    }
}

/// Replace surface `si`'s faces and rebuild its vertex list.
fn set_surface_faces(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &mut Classification, si: usize, faces: Vec<usize>) {
    let verts = vertices_of(mesh, &faces);
    match cls.selected[si] {
        SelectedSurface::Planar(i) => {
            cls.planar[i].faces = faces;
            cls.planar[i].vertices = verts;
        }
        SelectedSurface::Conical(i) => {
            cls.conical[i].faces = faces;
            cls.conical[i].vertices = verts;
        }
        SelectedSurface::Spherical(i) => {
            cls.spherical[i].faces = faces;
            cls.spherical[i].vertices = verts;
        }
        SelectedSurface::Toroidal(i) => {
            cls.toroidal[i].faces = faces;
            cls.toroidal[i].vertices = verts;
        }
        SelectedSurface::BSpline(_) => unreachable!("no bspline hypotheses in stage 2.3/2.4 yet"),
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cleanup + invariants
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/// Sort face lists and rebuild vertex lists for every surface.
fn rebuild_all(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &mut Classification) {
    for si in 0..cls.selected.len() {
        let mut faces = surface_faces(cls, si).to_vec();
        faces.sort_unstable();
        set_surface_faces(mesh, cls, si, faces);
    }
}

/// Drop selected surfaces that lost all their faces (e.g. merged away).
fn drop_empty_surfaces(cls: &mut Classification) {
    let keep: Vec<bool> = (0..cls.selected.len())
        .map(|si| !surface_faces(cls, si).is_empty())
        .collect();
    let mut si = 0;
    cls.selected.retain(|_| {
        let k = keep[si];
        si += 1;
        k
    });
}

/// Hard partition invariants required before stage 3.
fn assert_invariants(mesh: &ConnectedMesh, cls: &Classification) {
    let n = mesh.faces.len();

    // Total coverage: every face in exactly one selected surface.
    let mut count = vec![0u32; n];
    for si in 0..cls.selected.len() {
        for &fi in surface_faces(cls, si) {
            count[fi] += 1;
        }
    }
    for (fi, &c) in count.iter().enumerate() {
        assert_eq!(c, 1, "stage 2.4 invariant: face {fi} covered {c} times, expected 1");
    }

    // Connectivity: each surface's faces form one mesh-connected component.
    for si in 0..cls.selected.len() {
        let faces = surface_faces(cls, si);
        if faces.len() < 2 {
            continue;
        }
        let comps = connected_components(mesh, faces);
        assert_eq!(
            comps.len(),
            1,
            "stage 2.4 invariant: surface {si} has {} disconnected components",
            comps.len()
        );
    }
}
```

```
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::config;
    use crate::stage1::{
        ConnectedMesh, MeshFace, MeshVertex, UNDEDUCED_CONICAL_HYPOTHESIS,
        UNDEDUCED_PLANAR_HYPOTHESIS, UNDEDUCED_SPHERICAL_HYPOTHESIS, UNDEDUCED_TOROIDAL_HYPOTHESIS,
    };

    fn tri(a: usize, b: usize, c: usize) -> MeshFace {
        MeshFace {
            vertex_count: 3,
            vertex_indices: [a, b, c, 0],
            neighbors: [-1, -1, -1, -1],
            normal: None,
            planar_hypothesis: UNDEDUCED_PLANAR_HYPOTHESIS,
            spherical_hypothesis: UNDEDUCED_SPHERICAL_HYPOTHESIS,
            conical_hypothesis: UNDEDUCED_CONICAL_HYPOTHESIS,
            toroidal_hypothesis: UNDEDUCED_TOROIDAL_HYPOTHESIS,
        }
    }

    /// A connected strip of 3 coplanar quads (z=0), each split into two CCW
    /// (+z) triangles — 6 faces, all on the plane z=0.
    fn strip_mesh() -> ConnectedMesh {
        let verts = vec![
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(1.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(3.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(3.0, 1.0, 0.0),
        ];
        let faces = vec![
            tri(0, 2, 3), // quad0
            tri(0, 3, 1),
            tri(2, 4, 5), // quad1
            tri(2, 5, 3),
            tri(4, 6, 7), // quad2
            tri(4, 7, 5),
        ];
        let mut mesh = ConnectedMesh {
            vertices: verts,
            faces,
            stats: Default::default(),
        };
        mesh.validate_and_populate_topology().expect("valid mesh");
        mesh
    }

    fn planar(faces: Vec<usize>, verts: Vec<usize>) -> PlanarHypothesis {
        PlanarHypothesis {
            normal: [0.0, 0.0, 1.0],
            distance: 0.0,
            faces,
            vertices: verts,
            error_max: 0.0,
            error_min: 0.0,
            error_abs_sum: 0.0,
        }
    }

    fn test_config() -> config::Config {
        config::parse_config_from(["brepper", "dummy.stl", "-q"]).unwrap()
    }

    #[test]
    fn merge_absorbs_coplanar_small_region() {
        let mesh = strip_mesh();
        // T = quads 0+1 (4 faces), S = quad 2 (2 faces), all coplanar.
        let mut cls = Classification {
            planar: vec![
                planar(vec![0, 1, 2, 3], vec![0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]),
                planar(vec![4, 5], vec![4, 5, 6, 7]),
            ],
            conical: Vec::new(),
            spherical: Vec::new(),
            toroidal: Vec::new(),
            selected: vec![SelectedSurface::Planar(0), SelectedSurface::Planar(1)],
        };
        let stats = repair(&mesh, &mut cls, &test_config());
        assert_eq!(stats.merged, 1, "the coplanar small region should merge");
        assert_eq!(cls.selected.len(), 1, "one surface remains after merge");
        let faces = surface_faces(&cls, 0);
        assert_eq!(faces.len(), 6, "merged surface covers all 6 faces");
    }

    #[test]
    fn merge_leaves_perpendicular_region_alone() {
        // Same faces, but S is (falsely) a plane with a normal that does not
        // contain quad 2's vertices — model it by placing S's plane far away
        // so T does NOT fit S's vertices. Here instead we verify the guard by
        // making T small: a 2-face T must not be a merge target.
        let mesh = strip_mesh();
        let mut cls = Classification {
            planar: vec![
                planar(vec![0, 1], vec![0, 1, 2, 3]),       // 2-face T (too small)
                planar(vec![2, 3, 4, 5], vec![2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]),
            ],
            conical: Vec::new(),
            spherical: Vec::new(),
            toroidal: Vec::new(),
            selected: vec![SelectedSurface::Planar(0), SelectedSurface::Planar(1)],
        };
        let stats = repair(&mesh, &mut cls, &test_config());
        // The 2-face region can still merge INTO the 4-face one (T=4 faces
        // qualifies), so exactly one merge and one surface remain — coverage
        // and connectivity must hold either way.
        assert!(stats.merged <= 1);
        assert_invariants(&mesh, &cls);
    }

    #[test]
    fn resplit_breaks_disconnected_surface() {
        let mesh = strip_mesh();
        // One "surface" owning quad0 (faces 0,1) and quad2 (faces 4,5), which
        // are NOT mesh-adjacent (quad1 sits between them). Re-split must break
        // it into two connected surfaces.
        let mut cls = Classification {
            planar: vec![
                planar(vec![0, 1, 4, 5], vec![0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]),
                planar(vec![2, 3], vec![2, 3, 4, 5]),
            ],
            conical: Vec::new(),
            spherical: Vec::new(),
            toroidal: Vec::new(),
            selected: vec![SelectedSurface::Planar(0), SelectedSurface::Planar(1)],
        };
        let extra = resplit_disconnected(&mesh, &mut cls);
        assert_eq!(extra, 1, "the disconnected surface splits into two");
        // After the split every surface is one connected component.
        for si in 0..cls.selected.len() {
            let faces = surface_faces(&cls, si);
            if faces.len() >= 2 {
                assert_eq!(connected_components(&mesh, faces).len(), 1);
            }
        }
    }
}
```
</details>

```
    /// Two horizontal coplanar quads (z=0) plus one vertical quad (x=2)
    /// sharing their far edge — an L. Faces 0..3 lie on z=0, faces 4..5 on
    /// x=2, all one connected mesh.
    fn l_mesh() -> ConnectedMesh {
        let verts = vec![
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(0.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(1.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 0.0, 1.0),
            MeshVertex::from_xyz(2.0, 1.0, 1.0),
        ];
        let faces = vec![
            tri(0, 1, 2), // quad0, z=0
            tri(0, 2, 3),
            tri(1, 4, 5), // quad1, z=0
            tri(1, 5, 2),
            tri(4, 5, 7), // quad2, x=2 (vertical)
            tri(4, 7, 6),
        ];
        let mut mesh = ConnectedMesh {
            vertices: verts,
            faces,
            stats: Default::default(),
        };
        mesh.validate_and_populate_topology().expect("valid mesh");
        mesh
    }

    #[test]
    fn merge_leaves_perpendicular_region_alone() {
        let mesh = l_mesh();
        // T = the horizontal plane (4 faces), S = the vertical plane (2
        // faces). S shares an edge with T but its top vertices sit 1 mm off
        // T's plane, so the vertex-tolerance check must reject the merge.
        let mut cls = Classification {
            planar: vec![
                planar(vec![0, 1, 2, 3], vec![0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5]),

                    normal: [1.0, 0.0, 0.0],
                    distance: 2.0,
                    faces: vec![4, 5],
                    vertices: vec![4, 5, 6, 7],
                    error_max: 0.0,
                    error_min: 0.0,
                    error_abs_sum: 0.0,
                },
            ],
            conical: Vec::new(),
            spherical: Vec::new(),
            toroidal: Vec::new(),
            selected: vec![SelectedSurface::Planar(0), SelectedSurface::Planar(1)],
        };
        let stats = repair(&mesh, &mut cls, &test_config());
        assert_eq!(stats.merged, 0, "perpendicular planes must not merge");
        assert_eq!(cls.selected.len(), 2, "both surfaces survive");
        assert_invariants(&mesh, &cls);
    }
```
